new file mode 100644

+"""Synthetic daily-mart demonstration. Never used as pipeline output."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from datetime import date, timedelta
+import math
+
+import pandas as pd
+import plotly.graph_objects as go
+
+from dashboard.weather_case_study import chart_style
+
+SAMPLE_NOTICE = "Sample data for demonstration. Live pipeline output is not published."
+CITY_COLORS = {"Cairo": "#B45309", "Alexandria": "#1E3A5F"}
+
+
+def sample_daily_mart() -> pd.DataFrame:
+    """Fixed synthetic values shaped like mart_weather_daily, not API forecasts.
+
+    Jan 1-15, 2026 is an arbitrary demonstration window. Deterministic waves
+    illustrate filters and charts, not climate, accuracy, or real conditions.
+    """
+    rows = []
+    for city_index, city in enumerate(CITY_COLORS):
+        for day_index in range(15):
+            avg = round(17 - city_index * 2 + 2 * math.sin(day_index * .6 + city_index), 2)
+            rain = round((1.2 + city_index * 1.7) if day_index in (3, 7, 8, 12) else 0, 2)
+            humidity = round(55 + city_index * 12 + 8 * math.sin(day_index * .4), 2)
+            wind = round(13 + city_index * 4 + 3 * math.cos(day_index * .5), 2)
+            rows.append({
+                "city": city, "day": date(2026, 1, 1) + timedelta(days=day_index),
+                "hours_count": 22 if day_index == 0 else 2 if day_index == 14 else 24,
+                "avg_temp": avg, "min_temp": round(avg - 4, 2), "max_temp": round(avg + 5, 2),
+                "total_precipitation": rain,
+                "avg_humidity": humidity, "min_humidity": round(humidity - 10, 2), "max_humidity": round(humidity + 10, 2),
+                "avg_wind_speed": wind, "min_wind_speed": round(wind - 4, 2), "max_wind_speed": round(wind + 5, 2),
+                "avg_cloud_cover": round(30 + 20 * (1 + math.sin(day_index * .5 + city_index)), 2),
+            })
+    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
+
+
+def filter_sample(data: pd.DataFrame, cities: list[str], start: date, end: date, full_days: bool = False) -> pd.DataFrame:
+    mask = data['city'].isin(cities) & data['day'].between(start, end)
+    if full_days:
+        mask &= data['hours_count'].eq(24)
+    return data.loc[mask].copy()
+
+
+def temperature_chart(data: pd.DataFrame) -> go.Figure:
+    figure = go.Figure()
+    for city, color in CITY_COLORS.items():
+        subset = data.loc[data['city'].eq(city)]
+        if subset.empty:
+            continue
+        figure.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=subset['day'], y=subset['min_temp'], mode='lines', line={'width':0}, legendgroup=city, showlegend=False, hoverinfo='skip'))
+        figure.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=subset['day'], y=subset['max_temp'], mode='lines', line={'width':0}, fill='tonexty', fillcolor='rgba(180,83,9,0.10)' if city=='Cairo' else 'rgba(30,58,95,0.10)', legendgroup=city, showlegend=False, hoverinfo='skip'))
+        figure.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=subset['day'], y=subset['avg_temp'], mode='lines+markers', name=city, line={'color':color,'width':3}, marker={'size':5}, legendgroup=city, customdata=subset[['min_temp','max_temp','hours_count']].values, hovertemplate=city+' · %{x}<br>Avg %{y:.1f} °C<br>Min %{customdata[0]:.1f} / max %{customdata[1]:.1f} °C<br>Coverage %{customdata[2]} hours<extra>Sample</extra>'))
+    chart_style(figure, height=340)
+    figure.update_layout(showlegend=True, legend={'orientation':'h','y':1.16,'x':0}, hovermode='x unified')
+    figure.update_yaxes(title='Temperature (°C)')
+    figure.update_xaxes(tickformat='%d %b', nticks=5)
+    return figure
+
+
+def precipitation_chart(data: pd.DataFrame) -> go.Figure:
+    figure=go.Figure()
+    for city, color in CITY_COLORS.items():
+        subset=data.loc[data['city'].eq(city)]
+        if not subset.empty:
+            figure.add_trace(go.Bar(x=subset['day'],y=subset['total_precipitation'], name=city, marker_color=color, hovertemplate=city+' · %{x}<br>%{y:.1f} mm<extra>Sample</extra>'))
+    chart_style(figure, height=300)
+    figure.update_layout(barmode='group', showlegend=True, legend={'orientation':'h','y':1.16,'x':0})
+    figure.update_yaxes(title='Precipitation (mm)',rangemode='tozero')
+    figure.update_xaxes(tickformat='%d %b',nticks=5)
+    return figure

diff --git a/tests/test_weather_demo.py b/tests/test_weather_demo.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7b2b497
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_weather_demo.py
@@ -0,0 +1,47 @@
+from __future__ import annotations
+import unittest
+from datetime import date
+from pathlib import Path
+from dashboard.weather_demo import sample_daily_mart, filter_sample, temperature_chart, precipitation_chart
+from ui.navigation import SIDEBAR_PROJECTS
+from ui.project_cards import project_card_html
+from portfolio.content.projects import PROJECTS
+
+class WeatherDemoTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_sample_is_deterministic_at_city_day_grain(self):
+        data=sample_daily_mart()
+        self.assertTrue(data.equals(sample_daily_mart()))
+        self.assertEqual(len(data),30)
+        self.assertFalse(data.duplicated(['city','day']).any())
+        self.assertTrue((data.min_temp <= data.avg_temp).all())
+        self.assertTrue((data.avg_temp <= data.max_temp).all())
+        self.assertEqual(set(data.hours_count),{2,22,24})
+        self.assertTrue(data.total_precipitation.ge(0).all())
+
+    def test_filters_include_boundaries_and_empty_city_selection(self):
+        data=sample_daily_mart()
+        sample=filter_sample(data,['Cairo'],date(2026,1,1),date(2026,1,5))
+        self.assertEqual(len(sample),5)
+        self.assertEqual(len(filter_sample(data,['Cairo'],date(2026,1,1),date(2026,1,5),True)),4)
+        self.assertTrue(filter_sample(data,[],date(2026,1,1),date(2026,1,15)).empty)
+
+    def test_charts_follow_selected_city(self):
+        selected=sample_daily_mart().query('city == "Cairo"')
+        temperature=temperature_chart(selected)
+        self.assertEqual(len(temperature.data),3)
+        self.assertEqual(list(temperature.data[-1].y),selected.avg_temp.tolist())
+        rain=precipitation_chart(selected)
+        self.assertEqual(len(rain.data),1)
+        self.assertEqual(list(rain.data[0].y),selected.total_precipitation.tolist())
+
+    def test_dashboard_route_and_card_are_honest(self):
+        weather=next(p for p in PROJECTS if p['slug']=='weather-forecast-data-platform')
+        self.assertTrue(any(p['route']=='/weather_dashboard' for p in SIDEBAR_PROJECTS[0]['pages']))
+        self.assertIn('Explore Dashboard (sample)',project_card_html(weather))
+        source=(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'views/weather_dashboard.py').read_text()
+        self.assertIn('All values below are synthetic',source)
+        self.assertNotIn('requests.get',source)
+        self.assertNotIn('read_sql',source)
+
+if __name__=='__main__':
+    unittest.main()

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import streamlit as st
+
+from dashboard.weather_demo import SAMPLE_NOTICE, sample_daily_mart, filter_sample, temperature_chart, precipitation_chart
+
+st.markdown('''<section class="wx-section-head"><p class="pa-eyebrow">WEATHER / INTERACTIVE DEMO</p><h1>Explore the daily mart.</h1><p>Temperature ranges, rainy city-days and coverage, with the same city/day grain as the weather project.</p></section>''', unsafe_allow_html=True)
+st.warning(SAMPLE_NOTICE)
+st.caption("All values below are synthetic, fixed examples, not historical weather, current forecasts or measured pipeline output. Dates are an arbitrary demonstration window in Cairo-local calendar time.")
+st.page_link("views/weather_pipeline.py", label="Back to the pipeline case study", icon="↩️")
+data = sample_daily_mart()
+with st.container(border=True):
+    cities = st.multiselect("Cities", ["Cairo", "Alexandria"], default=["Cairo", "Alexandria"])
+    dates = st.date_input("Cairo-local date range (sample)", value=(data['day'].min(), data['day'].max()), min_value=data['day'].min(), max_value=data['day'].max(), format="DD/MM/YYYY")
+    full_days = st.checkbox("Only 24-hour sample city-days", value=False)
+    st.caption("Boundary city-days deliberately have 22 or 2 hours to demonstrate partial coverage. This filter keeps exactly 24 hours; it is not a general rule for DST days.")
+if not isinstance(dates, (list,tuple)) or len(dates) != 2:
+    st.info("Choose both a start and end date.")
+    st.stop()
+filtered = filter_sample(data, cities, dates[0], dates[1], full_days)
+if filtered.empty:
+    st.info("No sample city-days match these filters. Select a city or widen the date range.")
+    st.stop()
+mean = filtered['avg_temp'].mean()
+rainy = int(filtered['total_precipitation'].gt(0).sum())
+partial = int(filtered['hours_count'].ne(24).sum())
+st.markdown(f'''<div class="wx-metrics"><div><span>SAMPLE CITY-DAYS</span><strong>{len(filtered)}</strong><p>City × local calendar day</p></div><div><span>MEAN DAILY AVG</span><strong>{mean:.1f} °C</strong><p>Unweighted across selection</p></div><div><span>RAINY CITY-DAYS</span><strong>{rainy}</strong><p>Precipitation greater than 0 mm</p></div><div><span>PARTIAL CITY-DAYS</span><strong>{partial}</strong><p>Not 24 hours in this sample</p></div></div>''', unsafe_allow_html=True)
+st.subheader("Sample temperature trend")
+st.caption("Line = daily average. Shaded band = daily minimum to maximum. Hover to inspect min/max and hours_count. Partial-day averages do not have the same coverage as full days.")
+st.plotly_chart(temperature_chart(filtered), use_container_width=True, config={"displayModeBar":False})
+st.subheader("Sample precipitation by day")
+st.caption("Daily totals in millimeters. These are invented demonstration values, not observed rainfall. Rainy city-days count each city separately.")
+st.plotly_chart(precipitation_chart(filtered), use_container_width=True, config={"displayModeBar":False})
+with st.expander("Inspect sample daily-mart rows and download CSV"):
+    st.caption("Synthetic fields follow the repository mart column names. A future real export should carry its source, extraction time and forecast run date; replacing this sample is not a live database connection.")
+    st.dataframe(filtered, hide_index=True, use_container_width=True)
+    st.download_button("Download filtered SAMPLE CSV", data=filtered.to_csv(index=False), file_name="weather_SAMPLE_daily_mart.csv", mime="text/csv")
+st.markdown('<div class="wx-evidence-note"><strong>Demo only.</strong> The real project implements ingestion, models and orchestration. This portfolio dashboard is a separate sample-data interface. It does not query Open-Meteo, PostgreSQL, Airflow or AWS.</div>',unsafe_allow_html=True)
